# SOTN construct-precedent index

Machine-generated by `build_sotn_index.py`. Every entry is precedent from the
**sotn-decomp master branch** at:

- **Commit:** `aa53500226ee84be763f3e8702b27de06456b3a7`
- **Commit date:** 2026-07-10T08:18:04+10:00
- **Scanned:** 1911 C/H source files under `src/` (excluding `src/pc/`, the modern PC re-implementation)
- **Index generated:** 2026-09-01

Purpose: BB2's frozen sanctioned-family list requires SOTN-master evidence for every
matching-technique exception. Grep this file for a construct instead of re-researching SOTN.
A hit here is evidence the construct SHIPS in SOTN master; it is not, by itself, a BB2 authorization.

**Platform tags:** entries under `src/main_psp/` and `src/dra_psp/` are the PSP port (mwcc)
and `src/saturn/` is the Saturn port - different compilers from BB2's GCC 2.7.2, so they are
WEAKER precedent and are tagged `[PSP/mwcc]` / `[SATURN]`. Untagged entries are the PSX
(GCC 2.7.2) sources and rank first within each class.

## Counts

| class | construct | detection | count |
|---|---|---|---:|
| `fake_comment` | FAKE annotations (comments) | case-insensitive `fake` inside `//` or block comment text | 75 |
| `fake_identifier` | FAKE annotations (identifiers) | identifier containing `fake` outside comments (`color_fake`, `SsVabFakeHead`) | 194 |
| `self_assign` | Self-assignments | `x = x;` with textually identical lvalue/rvalue (incl. `->` / `[]` paths) | 13 |
| `match_comment` | Codegen-reason comments (dead stores / hacks) | comment asserting needed-for / to-match / hack / regalloc / scheduler / permuter | 229 |
| `do_while_zero` | `do { ... } while (0)` wraps | `while (0);` closer, with backward brace-balance to its `do {` | 18 |
| `pad_dummy_local` | Pad / dummy / unused locals | local decl named pad*/dummy*/unused*/spacer*/sp*/zero..nine (volatile qualifier noted) | 816 |
| `new_var_temp` | RA / scheduling temporaries (EXTRA class) | locals named `new_var*` or `phi*` - the decompiler-era temporaries SOTN keeps to hold register allocation in place | 20 |
| `pointer_alias` | Pointer aliases to globals | `T* p = &GLOBAL;` local alias of a global (trailing comment appended when present) | 206 |
| `dup_if_else_arm` | Duplicated statement in both if/else arms | identical non-trivial statement line present in both arms of one if/else | 958 |
| `const_holder` | Constant-holder locals carrying a comment | `sNN name = <literal>;` that has a comment on the same line | 41 |
| `empty_if` | Empty-body / fabricated conditionals | `if (cond) { }` / `if (cond) ;`, plus the multi-line form whose block closes immediately | 17 |
| `nested_exit_label` | Shared exit labels (mixed exits) | label declared at brace depth > 1 that is the target of a `goto` | 46 |

Classes with at most 1000 hits are listed IN FULL. Larger classes are capped at 1000 entries,
ordered **annotated-first** (hits whose source line carries a comment, i.e. the ones a
reader can cite as intent) then by path/line. The full count appears in every section
header, so nothing is silently truncated; re-run with a larger `--cap` to see the rest.
